(*=== APT111 ===*)

(* FUNCTION BLOCK: FB_HMI_EXCH *)

FUNCTION_BLOCK FB_HMI_EXCH

gI := 1;
IF First THEN gD := NOT gD; END_IF;
(* Valves *)
FOR i := 1 TO 48 DO
	exchV(i);
	(* Задвижка Э-1..Э-48 *)
END_FOR;
(* Discretes *)
FOR i := 2 TO 1 BY -1 DO
	exchD(i);
	(* Состояние двери АПТ 1.1.1 *)
	(* Наличие питания на вводе *)
END_FOR;
(* Connection *)
w := CON111_5;		exchWord(w);
w := CON111_112;	exchWord(w);
(* Diagnostics *)
CPU_pri[1] := __SYSVA_FO_ISPRIMARY;
CPU_act[1] := __SYSVA_FO_ISACTIVE;
CPU_pri[2] := NOT __SYSVA_FO_ISPRIMARY;
CPU_act[2] := NOT __SYSVA_FO_ISACTIVE;
exchMdlPSU(	inMdl := _111_G2_1_MDL,	inCh := _111_G2_1_PSU);
exchMdlPSU(	inMdl := _111_G2_2_MDL,	inCh := _111_G2_2_PSU);
exchMdlCPU(	inMdl := _111_A1_1_MDL,	isPri := CPU_pri[1],	isAct := CPU_act[1]);
exchMdlCPU(	inMdl := _111_A1_2_MDL,	isPri := CPU_pri[2],	isAct := CPU_act[2]);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _111_A2_MDl,	inCh := _111_A2_DI);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _111_A3_MDl,	inCh := _111_A3_DI);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _111_A4_MDl,	inCh := _111_A4_DO);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _111_A5_MDl,	inCh := _111_A5_DO);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _111_A6_MDl,	inCh := _111_A6_DI);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _111_A7_MDl,	inCh := _111_A7_DI);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _111_A8_MDl,	inCh := _111_A8_DO);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _111_A9_MDl,	inCh := _111_A9_DO);
END_FUNCTION_BLOCK
(* -------------------- *)

(* ROUTINE 03: _111_Inst *)

PROGRAM _111_Inst

(* Valves *)
FOR i := 1 TO 48 DO
	_gVLV(i);
	(* Задвижка Э-1..Э-48 *)
END_FOR;

(* Discretes *)
FOR i := 1 TO 2 DO
	_gDI(i);
	(* Состояние двери АПТ 1.1.1 *)
	(* Наличие питания на вводе *)
END_FOR;
END_PROGRAM

(* Пожар на резервуаре 1/8 *)
v[1] := 25;

v[3] := 27;

v[9] := 0;
v[10] := 0;
vr[1] := 24;

vr[3] := 29;
vr[4] := 30;
vlv(g := i,	v := v,	vr := vr); i:=i+1;
(* Автоматическое управление задвижками *)
b := VlvAut(v := 1, fs1 := 1, fs2 := 0);
b := VlvAut(v := 2, fs1 := 1, fs2 := 0);

(* ROUTINE 05: _111_RTU_Master *)

PROGRAM _111_RTU_Master

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First OR sInit THEN
	sInit := FALSE;
	
	(* 48 identical valves on 2 ports; 24 valves on each port; 2 requests for each valve: read, write; 48 requests on each port *)
	(* available: 8 request groups on each port, 16 requests in each group *)
	sMaxReqs := 16;
	sMaxCtrls := (24 * 2) / sMaxReqs; (* 24 = Number of Modbus Devices *)
	(* Preparing configs for requests *)
	FOR i := 1 TO 24 DO
		(* reading *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 1] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 1] := 3;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 1] := 4; (* 40004; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 1] := 4;
		(* writing *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 0] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 0] := 16;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 0] := 2; (* 40002; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 0] := 2;
	END_FOR;
	sOffset := 0;
	sReqNo := 1;
	sCtrlNo := 0;
	(* Configuring requests *)
	FOR i := 1 TO 48 DO
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].single_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].on_modify_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].do_single_req := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.timeout_ms := 1000;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.repeat_over_scan := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_id 			:= cfg_SID[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.func_code 		:= cfg_FUN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_addr	:= cfg_ADR[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_length	:= cfg_LEN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.offset := sOffset;
		sOffset := sOffset + cfg_LEN[i];
		sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
		IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
			sReqNo := 1;
			sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
			IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
				sCtrlNo := 0;
			END_IF;
		END_IF;
	END_FOR;
	_Ctrl_2 := _Ctrl_1;
	_Control_1 := 1;
	_Control_2 := 1;
END_IF;

(* Every Cycle *)
sCtrlNo := 0;
sReqNo := 1;
FOR i := 1 TO 24 DO
	(* 24 valves on Port 1 *)
	j:=i;
	sCOBA(
		iReqStatus	:= _Diag_1[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
		i40004 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 1],
		i40005 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 2],
		i40006 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 3],
		i40007 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 4],
		g		:= j,
		o40002	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 5],
		o40003	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 6]
	);
	(* 24 valves on Port 2 *)
	j:=i+24;
	sCOBA(
		iReqStatus	:= _Diag_2[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
		i40004 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 1],
		i40005 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 2],
		i40006 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 3],
		i40007 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 4],
		g		:= j,
		o40002	=> _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 5],
		o40003	=> _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 6]
	);
	sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
	IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
		sReqNo := 1;
		sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
		IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
			sCtrlNo := 0;
		END_IF;
	END_IF;
END_FOR;
END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)


(* ROUTINE 06: _111_TCP_Master *)

PROGRAM _111_TCP_Master

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First THEN
	_Control := 1;
	(*	En	IP					Port	ID	Func	RegNo			Len		Offset in _HR	Timeout	*)
	rq01(1,	'192.168.0.6'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 1] := rq01.out;	(* АПТ 5 основной *)
	rq02(0,	'192.168.1.6'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 2] := rq02.out;	(* АПТ 5 резервный *)
	rq03(1,	'192.168.0.2'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 3] := rq03.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.2 основной *)
	rq04(0,	'192.168.1.2'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 4] := rq04.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.2 резервный *)
	rq05(1,	'192.168.0.5'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1001		,	43	,	0				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 5] := rq05.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 основной *)
	rq06(0,	'192.168.1.5'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1001		,	43	,	0				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 6] := rq06.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 резервный *)
	rq07(1,	'192.168.0.5'	,	502	,	1,	16	,	1101+8*0	,	8	,	100				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 7] := rq07.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 основной *)
	rq08(0,	'192.168.1.5'	,	502	,	1,	16	,	1101+8*0	,	8	,	100				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 8] := rq08.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 резервный *)
END_IF;

sCtrlNo := 0;
(* 5 *)
sReqNo := 1;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON111_5 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON111_5 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON111_5 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON111_5 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 112 *)
sReqNo := 3;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON111_112 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON111_112 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON111_112 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON111_112 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 23 read *)
sReqNo := 5;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 23 write *)
sReqNo := 7;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;


(* Пожар резервуаров 1/1..1/8 *)
FOR i := 1 TO 8 DO
	gFS[i].PRM.FIRE := _HR[0].regs[i];
END_FOR;
(* Состояние клапанов *)
FOR i := 1 TO 8 DO
	_HR[0].regs[i + 100] := gFS[i].PRM.VLVS;
END_FOR;
END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)

PROGRAM _111_HMI_out

(* Transfering to HMI *)
hmiExch();
END_PROGRAM

(*=== APT112 ===*)

(* FUNCTION BLOCK: FB_HMI_EXCH *)

FUNCTION_BLOCK FB_HMI_EXCH

gI := 1;
IF First THEN gD := NOT gD; END_IF;
(* Valves *)
FOR i := 1 TO 48 DO
	exchV(i);
	(* Задвижка Э-49..Э-96 *)
END_FOR;
(* Discretes *)
FOR i := 2 TO 1 BY -1 DO
	exchD(i);	
	(* Состояние двери АПТ 1.1.2 *)
	(* Наличие питания на вводе *)
END_FOR;
(* Connection *)
w := CON112_111;	exchWord(w);
w := CON112_113;	exchWord(w);
gI := gI + 2;
(* Diagnostics *)
CPU_pri[1] := __SYSVA_FO_ISPRIMARY;
CPU_act[1] := __SYSVA_FO_ISACTIVE;
CPU_pri[2] := NOT __SYSVA_FO_ISPRIMARY;
CPU_act[2] := NOT __SYSVA_FO_ISACTIVE;
exchMdlPSU(	inMdl := _112_G2_1_MDL,	inCh := _112_G2_1_PSU);
exchMdlPSU(	inMdl := _112_G2_2_MDL,	inCh := _112_G2_2_PSU);
exchMdlCPU(	inMdl := _112_A1_1_MDL,	isPri := CPU_pri[1],	isAct := CPU_act[1]);
exchMdlCPU(	inMdl := _112_A1_2_MDL,	isPri := CPU_pri[2],	isAct := CPU_act[2]);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _112_A2_MDl,	inCh := _112_A2_DI);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _112_A3_MDl,	inCh := _112_A3_DI);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _112_A4_MDl,	inCh := _112_A4_DO);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _112_A5_MDl,	inCh := _112_A5_DO);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _112_A6_MDl,	inCh := _112_A6_DI);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _112_A7_MDl,	inCh := _112_A7_DI);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _112_A8_MDl,	inCh := _112_A8_DO);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _112_A9_MDl,	inCh := _112_A9_DO);
END_FUNCTION_BLOCK

(* ROUTINE 05: _112_RTU_Master *)

PROGRAM _112_RTU_Master

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First OR sInit THEN
	sInit := FALSE;
	
	(* 48 identical valves on 2 ports; 24 valves on each port; 2 requests for each valve: read, write; 48 requests on each port *)
	(* available: 8 request groups on each port, 16 requests in each group *)
	sMaxReqs := 16;
	sMaxCtrls := (24 * 2) / sMaxReqs; (* 24 = Number of Modbus Devices *)
	(* Preparing configs for requests *)
	FOR i := 1 TO 24 DO
		(* reading *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 1] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 1] := 3;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 1] := 4; (* 40004; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 1] := 4;
		(* writing *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 0] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 0] := 16;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 0] := 2; (* 40002; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 0] := 2;
	END_FOR;
	sOffset := 0;
	sReqNo := 1;
	sCtrlNo := 0;
	(* Configuring requests *)
	FOR i := 1 TO 48 DO
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].single_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].on_modify_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].do_single_req := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.timeout_ms := 1000;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.repeat_over_scan := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_id 			:= cfg_SID[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.func_code 		:= cfg_FUN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_addr	:= cfg_ADR[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_length	:= cfg_LEN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.offset := sOffset;
		sOffset := sOffset + cfg_LEN[i];
		sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
		IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
			sReqNo := 1;
			sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
			IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
				sCtrlNo := 0;
			END_IF;
		END_IF;
	END_FOR;
	_Ctrl_2 := _Ctrl_1;
	_Control_1 := 1;
	_Control_2 := 1;
END_IF;

(* Every Cycle *)
sCtrlNo := 0;
sReqNo := 1;
FOR i := 1 TO 24 DO
	(* 24 valves on Port 1 *)
	j:=i;
	sCOBA(
		iReqStatus	:= _Diag_1[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
		i40004 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 1],
		i40005 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 2],
		i40006 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 3],
		i40007 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 4],
		g		:= j,
		o40002	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 5],
		o40003	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 6]
	);
	(* 24 valves on Port 2 *)
	j:=i+24;
	sCOBA(
		iReqStatus	:= _Diag_2[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
		i40004 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 1],
		i40005 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 2],
		i40006 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 3],
		i40007 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 4],
		g		:= j,
		o40002	=> _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 5],
		o40003	=> _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 6]
	);
	sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
	IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
		sReqNo := 1;
		sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
		IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
			sCtrlNo := 0;
		END_IF;
	END_IF;
END_FOR;
END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)


(* ROUTINE 06: _112_TCP_Master *)

PROGRAM _112_TCP_Master

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First THEN
	_Control := 1;
	(*	En	IP					Port	ID	Func	RegNo			Len		Offset in _HR	Timeout	*)
	rq01(1,	'192.168.0.1'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 1] := rq01.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.1 основной *)
	rq02(0,	'192.168.1.1'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 2] := rq02.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.1 резервный *)
	rq03(1,	'192.168.0.3'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 3] := rq03.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.3 основной *)
	rq04(0,	'192.168.1.3'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 4] := rq04.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.3 резервный *)
	rq05(1,	'192.168.0.5'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1001		,	43	,	0				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 5] := rq05.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 основной *)
	rq06(0,	'192.168.1.5'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1001		,	43	,	0				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 6] := rq06.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 резервный *)
	rq07(1,	'192.168.0.5'	,	502	,	1,	16	,	1101+8*1	,	8	,	100				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 7] := rq07.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 основной *)
	rq08(0,	'192.168.1.5'	,	502	,	1,	16	,	1101+8*1	,	8	,	100				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 8] := rq08.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 резервный *)
END_IF;

sCtrlNo := 0;
(* 111 *)
sReqNo := 1;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON112_111 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON112_111 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON112_111 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON112_111 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 113 *)
sReqNo := 3;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON112_113 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON112_113 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON112_113 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON112_113 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 23 read *)
sReqNo := 5;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 23 write *)
sReqNo := 7;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;


(* Пожар резервуаров 1/9..1/16 *)
FOR i := 1 TO 8 DO
	gFS[i].PRM.FIRE := _HR[0].regs[i+8];
END_FOR;
(* Состояние клапанов *)
FOR i := 1 TO 8 DO
	_HR[0].regs[i + 100] := gFS[i].PRM.VLVS;
END_FOR;
END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)

(*=== APT113 ===*)

(* FUNCTION BLOCK: FB_HMI_EXCH *)

FUNCTION_BLOCK FB_HMI_EXCH

gI := 1;
IF First THEN gD := NOT gD; END_IF;
(* Valves *)
FOR i := 1 TO 48 DO
	exchV(i);
	(* Задвижка Э-97..Э-144 *)
END_FOR;
(* Discretes *)
FOR i := 2 TO 1 BY -1 DO
	exchD(i);
	(* Состояние двери АПТ 1.1.3 *)
	(* Наличие питания на вводе *)
END_FOR;
(* Connection *)
w := CON113_112;	exchWord(w);
w := CON113_114;	exchWord(w);
(* Diagnostics *)
CPU_pri[1] := __SYSVA_FO_ISPRIMARY;
CPU_act[1] := __SYSVA_FO_ISACTIVE;
CPU_pri[2] := NOT __SYSVA_FO_ISPRIMARY;
CPU_act[2] := NOT __SYSVA_FO_ISACTIVE;
exchMdlPSU(	inMdl := _113_G2_1_MDL,	inCh := _113_G2_1_PSU);
exchMdlPSU(	inMdl := _113_G2_2_MDL,	inCh := _113_G2_2_PSU);
exchMdlCPU(	inMdl := _113_A1_1_MDL,	isPri := CPU_pri[1],	isAct := CPU_act[1]);
exchMdlCPU(	inMdl := _113_A1_2_MDL,	isPri := CPU_pri[2],	isAct := CPU_act[2]);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _113_A2_MDl,	inCh := _113_A2_DI);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _113_A3_MDl,	inCh := _113_A3_DI);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _113_A4_MDl,	inCh := _113_A4_DO);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _113_A5_MDl,	inCh := _113_A5_DO);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _113_A6_MDl,	inCh := _113_A6_DI);
exchMdlDI(	inMdl := _113_A7_MDl,	inCh := _113_A7_DI);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _113_A8_MDl,	inCh := _113_A8_DO);
exchMdlDO(	inMdl := _113_A9_MDl,	inCh := _113_A9_DO);
END_FUNCTION_BLOCK

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First OR sInit THEN
	sInit := FALSE;
	
	(* 48 identical valves on 2 ports; 24 valves on each port; 2 requests for each valve: read, write; 48 requests on each port *)
	(* available: 8 request groups on each port, 16 requests in each group *)
	sMaxReqs := 16;
	sMaxCtrls := (24 * 2) / sMaxReqs; (* 24 = Number of Modbus Devices *)
	(* Preparing configs for requests *)
	FOR i := 1 TO 24 DO
		(* reading *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 1] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 1] := 3;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 1] := 4; (* 40004; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 1] := 4;
		(* writing *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 0] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 0] := 16;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 0] := 2; (* 40002; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 0] := 2;
	END_FOR;
	sOffset := 0;
	sReqNo := 1;
	sCtrlNo := 0;
	(* Configuring requests *)
	FOR i := 1 TO 48 DO
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].single_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].on_modify_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].do_single_req := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.timeout_ms := 1000;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.repeat_over_scan := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_id 			:= cfg_SID[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.func_code 		:= cfg_FUN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_addr	:= cfg_ADR[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_length	:= cfg_LEN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.offset := sOffset;
		sOffset := sOffset + cfg_LEN[i];
		sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
		IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
			sReqNo := 1;
			sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
			IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
				sCtrlNo := 0;
			END_IF;
		END_IF;
	END_FOR;
	_Ctrl_2 := _Ctrl_1;
	_Control_1 := 1;
	_Control_2 := 1;
END_IF;

(* Every Cycle *)
sCtrlNo := 0;
sReqNo := 1;
FOR i := 1 TO 24 DO
	(* 24 valves on Port 1 *)
	j:=i;
	sCOBA(
		iReqStatus	:= _Diag_1[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
		i40004 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 1],
		i40005 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 2],
		i40006 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 3],
		i40007 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 4],
		g		:= j,
		o40002	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 5],
		o40003	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 6]
	);
	(* 24 valves on Port 2 *)
	j:=i+24;
	sCOBA(
		iReqStatus	:= _Diag_2[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
		i40004 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 1],
		i40005 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 2],
		i40006 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 3],
		i40007 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 4],
		g		:= j,
		o40002	=> _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 5],
		o40003	=> _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 6]
	);
	sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
	IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
		sReqNo := 1;
		sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
		IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
			sCtrlNo := 0;
		END_IF;
	END_IF;
END_FOR;
END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)


(* ROUTINE 06: _113_TCP_Master *)

PROGRAM _113_TCP_Master

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First THEN
	_Control := 1;
	(*	En	IP					Port	ID	Func	RegNo			Len		Offset in _HR	Timeout	*)
	rq01(1,	'192.168.0.2'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 1] := rq01.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.2 основной *)
	rq02(0,	'192.168.1.2'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 2] := rq02.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.2 резервный *)
	rq03(1,	'192.168.0.4'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 3] := rq03.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.4 основной *)
	rq04(0,	'192.168.1.4'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 4] := rq04.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.4 резервный *)
	rq05(1,	'192.168.0.5'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1001		,	43	,	0				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 5] := rq05.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 основной *)
	rq06(0,	'192.168.1.5'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1001		,	43	,	0				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 6] := rq06.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 резервный *)
	rq07(1,	'192.168.0.5'	,	502	,	1,	16	,	1101+8*2	,	8	,	100				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 7] := rq07.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 основной *)
	rq08(0,	'192.168.1.5'	,	502	,	1,	16	,	1101+8*2	,	8	,	100				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 8] := rq08.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 резервный *)
END_IF;

sCtrlNo := 0;
(* 112 *)
sReqNo := 1;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON113_112 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON113_112 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON113_112 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON113_112 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 114 *)
sReqNo := 3;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON113_114 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON113_114 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON113_114 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON113_114 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 23 read *)
sReqNo := 5;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 23 write *)
sReqNo := 7;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;


(* Пожар резервуаров 1/17..1/24 *)
FOR i := 1 TO 8 DO
	gFS[i].PRM.FIRE := _HR[0].regs[i+8*2];
END_FOR;
(* Состояние клапанов *)
FOR i := 1 TO 8 DO
	_HR[0].regs[i + 100] := gFS[i].PRM.VLVS;
END_FOR;

END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)

hmiExch();
END_PROGRAM

(* FUNCTION BLOCK: FB_HMI_EXCH *)

FUNCTION_BLOCK FB_HMI_EXCH

gI := 1;
IF First THEN gD := NOT gD; END_IF;
(* Valves *)
FOR i := 1 TO 48 DO
	exchV(i);
	(* Задвижка Э-145..Э-192 *)
END_FOR;
(* Discretes *)
FOR i := 2 TO 1 BY -1 DO
	exchD(i);
	(* Состояние двери АПТ 1.1.4 *)
	(* Наличие питания на вводе *)
END_FOR;
(* Connection *)
w := CON114_113;	exchWord(w);
w := CON114_23;		exchWord(w);
(* Diagnostics *)
CPU_pri[1] := __SYSVA_FO_ISPRIMARY;
CPU_act[1] := __SYSVA_FO_ISACTIVE;
CPU_pri[2] := NOT __SYSVA_FO_ISPRIMARY;
CPU_act[2] := NOT __SYSVA_FO_ISACTIVE;
exchMdlPSU(	inMdl := _114_G2_1_MDL,	inCh := _114_G2_1_PSU);
exchMdlPSU(	inMdl := _114_G2_2_MDL,	inCh := _114_G2_2_PSU);

(* ROUTINE 05: _114_RTU_Master *)

PROGRAM _114_RTU_Master

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First OR sInit THEN
	sInit := FALSE;
	
	(* 48 identical valves on 2 ports; 24 valves on each port; 2 requests for each valve: read, write; 48 requests on each port *)
	(* available: 8 request groups on each port, 16 requests in each group *)
	sMaxReqs := 16;
	sMaxCtrls := (24 * 2) / sMaxReqs; (* 24 = Number of Modbus Devices *)
	(* Preparing configs for requests *)
	FOR i := 1 TO 24 DO
		(* reading *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 1] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 1] := 3;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 1] := 4; (* 40004; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 1] := 4;
		(* writing *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 0] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 0] := 16;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 0] := 2; (* 40002; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 0] := 2;
	END_FOR;
	sOffset := 0;
	sReqNo := 1;
	sCtrlNo := 0;
	(* Configuring requests *)
	FOR i := 1 TO 48 DO
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].single_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].on_modify_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].do_single_req := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.timeout_ms := 1000;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.repeat_over_scan := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_id 			:= cfg_SID[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.func_code 		:= cfg_FUN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_addr	:= cfg_ADR[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_length	:= cfg_LEN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.offset := sOffset;
		sOffset := sOffset + cfg_LEN[i];
		sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
		IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
			sReqNo := 1;
			sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
			IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
				sCtrlNo := 0;
			END_IF;
		END_IF;
	END_FOR;
	_Ctrl_2 := _Ctrl_1;
	_Control_1 := 1;
	_Control_2 := 1;
END_IF;

(* Every Cycle *)
sCtrlNo := 0;
sReqNo := 1;
FOR i := 1 TO 24 DO
	(* 24 valves on Port 1 *)
	j:=i;
	sCOBA(
		iReqStatus	:= _Diag_1[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
		i40004 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 1],
		i40005 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 2],
		i40006 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 3],
		i40007 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 4],
		g		:= j,
		o40002	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 5],
		o40003	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 6]
	);
	(* 24 valves on Port 2 *)
	j:=i+24;
	sCOBA(
		iReqStatus	:= _Diag_2[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
		i40004 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 1],
		i40005 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 2],
		i40006 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 3],
		i40007 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 4],
		g		:= j,
		o40002	=> _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 5],
		o40003	=> _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 6]
	);
	sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
	IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
		sReqNo := 1;
		sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
		IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
			sCtrlNo := 0;
		END_IF;
	END_IF;
END_FOR;
END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)


(* ROUTINE 06: _114_TCP_Master *)

PROGRAM _114_TCP_Master

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First THEN
	_Control := 1;
	(*	En	IP					Port	ID	Func	RegNo			Len		Offset in _HR	Timeout	*)
	rq01(1,	'192.168.0.3'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 1] := rq01.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.3 основной *)
	rq02(0,	'192.168.1.3'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 2] := rq02.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.3 резервный *)
	rq03(1,	'192.168.0.5'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1001		,	43	,	0				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 5] := rq03.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 основной *)
	rq04(0,	'192.168.1.5'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1001		,	43	,	0				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 6] := rq04.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 резервный *)
	rq05(1,	'192.168.0.5'	,	502	,	1,	16	,	1101+8*3	,	8	,	100				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 7] := rq05.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 основной *)
	rq06(0,	'192.168.1.5'	,	502	,	1,	16	,	1101+8*3	,	8	,	100				,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 8] := rq06.out;	(* АПТ 2.3 резервный *)
END_IF;

sCtrlNo := 0;
(* 113 *)
sReqNo := 1;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON114_113 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON114_113 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON114_113 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON114_113 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 23 read *)
sReqNo := 5;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON114_23 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON114_23 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON114_23 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON114_23 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 23 write *)
sReqNo := 7;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		;
	END_IF;
END_IF;


(* Пожар резервуаров 1/25..1/32 *)
FOR i := 1 TO 8 DO
	gFS[i].PRM.FIRE := _HR[0].regs[i+8*3];
END_FOR;
(* Состояние клапанов *)
FOR i := 1 TO 8 DO
	_HR[0].regs[i + 100] := gFS[i].PRM.VLVS;
END_FOR;


END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)

PROGRAM _114_HMI_out

hmiExch();
END_PROGRAM

gVLV[11].in.LOCAL  		:= _023_A3_DI[28].input;	(* Зд Э-203 Ручной режим *)
gVLV[11].in.REMOTE 		:= _023_A3_DI[29].input;	(* Зд Э-203 Дистанционный режим *)
gDI[3].IN				:= _023_A3_DI[31].input;	gDI[3].MDL := _mdl; (* Состояние двери АПТ 2.3 *)
(* A4 *)
_mdl := MdlErr(_023_A4_MDL);
gDI[1].IN 				:= _023_A4_DI[0].input;		gDI[1].MDL := _mdl; (* Наличие питания на вводе 1 *)
gDI[2].IN 				:= _023_A4_DI[1].input;		gDI[2].MDL := _mdl; (* Наличие питания на вводе 2 *)
_dis					:= _023_A4_DI[2].input;		(*  *)

_dis					:= _023_A4_DI[13].input;	(*  *)
_dis					:= _023_A4_DI[14].input;	(*  *)
_dis					:= _023_A4_DI[15].input;	(*  *)
_dis					:= _023_A4_DI[16].input;	(*  *)

	(* 11 A10.K11	Пожар резервуара 1/11 *)

	(* 13 A11.K1	Пожар резервуара 1/13 *)
	(* 14 A11.K2	Пожар резервуара 1/14 *)
	(* 15 A11.K3	Пожар резервуара 1/15 *)

(* ROUTINE 05: _23_RTU_Master *)

PROGRAM _23_RTU_Master

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First OR sInit THEN
	sInit := FALSE;
	
	(* 11 identical valves on Port 1; 4 12-channel COBA on Port 2 *)
	(* available: 8 request groups on each port, 16 requests in each group *)
	sMaxReqs := 16;
	sMaxCtrls := (11 * 2) / sMaxReqs; (* 24 = Number of Modbus Devices *)
	(* Preparing configs for requests *)
	FOR i := 1 TO 11 DO
		(* reading *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 1] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 1] := 3;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 1] := 4; (* 40004; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 1] := 4;
		(* writing *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 0] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 0] := 16;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 0] := 2; (* 40002; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 0] := 2;
	END_FOR;
	FOR i := 1 TO 4 DO
		(* reading *)
		cfg2_SID[i*2 - 1] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg2_FUN[i*2 - 1] := 3;
		cfg2_ADR[i*2 - 1] := 3; (* 40003..40014; *)
		cfg2_LEN[i*2 - 1] := 12;
		(* writing *)
		cfg2_SID[i*2 - 0] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg2_FUN[i*2 - 0] := 16;
		cfg2_ADR[i*2 - 0] := 2; (* 40002; *)
		cfg2_LEN[i*2 - 0] := 1;
	END_FOR;
	(* Configuring requests *)
	sOffset := 0;
	sReqNo := 1;
	sCtrlNo := 0;
	FOR i := 1 TO 22 DO
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].single_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].on_modify_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].do_single_req := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.timeout_ms := 1000;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.repeat_over_scan := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_id 			:= cfg_SID[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.func_code 		:= cfg_FUN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_addr	:= cfg_ADR[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_length	:= cfg_LEN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.offset := sOffset;
		sOffset := sOffset + cfg_LEN[i];
		sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
		IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
			sReqNo := 1;
			sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
			IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
				sCtrlNo := 0;
			END_IF;
		END_IF;
	END_FOR;
	sOffset := 0;
	sReqNo := 1;
	sCtrlNo := 0;
	FOR i := 1 TO 8 DO
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].single_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].on_modify_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].do_single_req := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.timeout_ms := 1000;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.repeat_over_scan := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_id 			:= cfg_SID[i];
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.func_code 		:= cfg_FUN[i];
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_addr	:= cfg_ADR[i];
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_length	:= cfg_LEN[i];
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.offset := sOffset;
		sOffset := sOffset + cfg_LEN[i];
		sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
		IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
			sReqNo := 1;
			sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
			IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
				sCtrlNo := 0;
			END_IF;
		END_IF;
	END_FOR;
	_Control_1 := 1;
	_Control_2 := 1;
END_IF;

(* Every Cycle *)
sCtrlNo := 0;
sReqNo := 1;
(* 11 valves on Port 1 *)
FOR i := 1 TO 11 DO
	j:=i;
	sCOBA_VLV(
		iReqStatus	:= _Diag_1[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
		i40004 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 1],
		i40005 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 2],
		i40006 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 3],
		i40007 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 4],
		g		:= j,
		o40002	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 5],
		o40003	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 6]
	);
	sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
	IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
		sReqNo := 1;
		sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
		IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
			sCtrlNo := 0;
		END_IF;
	END_IF;
END_FOR;
(* 4 12-channel COBA on Port 2 *)
FOR i := 1 TO 4 DO
	FOR j := 1 TO 12 DO
		IF i=4 AND j>8 THEN EXIT; END_IF;
		sCOBA_DIS(
			iReqStatus	:= _Diag_2[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
			iBit		:= j-1,
			iStatus 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*13 + j],
			g			:= i*j + 3,
			o40002		=> _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*13 + 13]
		);
	END_FOR;
	sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
	IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
		sReqNo := 1;
		sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
		IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
			sCtrlNo := 0;
		END_IF;
	END_IF;
END_FOR;
END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)


(* ROUTINE 06: _23_TCP_Master *)

PROGRAM _23_TCP_Master

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First THEN
	_Control := 1;
	(*	En	IP					Port	ID	Func	RegNo			Len		Offset in _HR	Timeout	*)
	rq01(1,	'192.168.0.4'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 1] := rq01.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.4 основной *)
	rq02(0,	'192.168.1.4'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 2] := rq02.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.4 резервный *)
	rq03(1,	'192.168.0.6'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 3] := rq03.out;	(* АПТ 5 основной *)
	rq04(0,	'192.168.1.6'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 4] := rq04.out;	(* АПТ 5 резервный *)
END_IF;

sCtrlNo := 0;
(* 114 *)
sReqNo := 1;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON23_114 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON23_114 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON23_114 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON23_114 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
(* 5 *)
sReqNo := 3;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON23_5 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON23_5 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
IF _Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable THEN
	IF _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>0 OR _Diag[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo+1].status_request<>100 THEN
		CON23_5 := 0;
		IF Timer[sReqNo+1]<3000 THEN
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := Timer[sReqNo+1] + CYCLE_TIME;
		ELSE
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo+1].enable := FALSE;
			_Ctrl[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
			Timer[sReqNo+1] := 0;
		END_IF;
	ELSE
		CON23_5 := 1;
	END_IF;
END_IF;
END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)


(* ROUTINE 07: _23_HMI_out *)

PROGRAM _23_HMI_out

(* Transfering data to HMI *)
hmiExch();
END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)

(* FUNCTION BLOCK: FB_HMI_EXCH *)

FUNCTION_BLOCK FB_HMI_EXCH

gI := 1;
IF First THEN gD := NOT gD; END_IF;
(* Valves *)

	exchV(i);
	(* 1	Задвижка Э-1 *)
	(* 2	Задвижка Э-2 *)
	(* 3	Задвижка Э-3 *)
	(* 4	Задвижка Э-4 *)

	(* 9	Задвижка Э-9 *)
	(* 10	Задвижка Э-10 *)

	exchC(i);
	(* 1	ШУПН 1 *)
	(* 2	ШУПН 2 *)
	(* 3	ШУПН 3 *)
END_FOR;
(* Pumps *)

	exchP(i);
	(* 1	Насос Н-1/1 *)
	(* 2	Насос Н-1/2 *)
	(* 3	Насос Н-2/1 *)
	(* 4	Насос Н-2/2 *)
	(* 5	Насос Н-3/1 *)
	(* 6	Насос Н-3/2 *)
	(* 7	БДП *)
END_FOR;
(* Analog parameters *)
FOR i := 1 TO 35 DO
	exchT(i);
	(* 1	LT01 В пож резервуаре ПР1 *)
	(* 2	TT01 В пож резервуаре ПР1 *)
	(* 3	LT02 В пож резервуаре ПР2 *)

	(* 12	N11_TT_B1 подшипника №1 Н1.1 *)
	(* 13	N11_TT_B2 подшипника №2 Н1.1 *)
	(* 14	N12_TT_B1 подшипника №1 Н1.2 *)
	(* 15	N12_TT_B2 подшипника №2 Н1.2 *)

	(* 21	N31_TT_B2 подшипника №2 Н3.1 *)
	(* 22	N32_TT_B1 подшипника №1 Н3.2 *)

	(* 35	N32_TE_BM2 подшипника эд №2 Н3.2 *)

exchMdlAI(	inMdl := _005_A5_MDl,	inCh := _005_A5_AI);

(* Interconnections *)
END_FUNCTION_BLOCK

(* ROUTINE 04: _5_Inst *)

PROGRAM _5_Inst
(* Valves *)
FOR i := 7 TO 10 DO
	_gVLV(i);
	(* 7	Задвижка Э-7 *)
	(* 8	Задвижка Э-8 *)
	(* 9	Задвижка Э-9 *)
	(* 10	Задвижка Э-10 *)
END_FOR;
(* Pumps *)
i := 7;
_gACT(i);
(* 7	БДП *)

(* Analog parameters *)
FOR i := 1 TO 35 DO
	_gAI(i);
	(* 1	LT01 В пож резервуаре ПР1 *)
	(* 2	TT01 В пож резервуаре ПР1 *)
	(* 3	LT02 В пож резервуаре ПР2 *)
	(* 4	TT02 В пож резервуаре ПР2 *)
	(* 5	PT01 после насосов Н1.1/Н1.2 В2 *)
	(* 6	PT02 после насосов Н2.1/Н2.2 В10 *)
	(* 7	PT03 после насосов Н3.1/Н3.2 В2 *)
	(* 8	PT04 в сети B10 после задвижки Э-07 *)
	(* 9	PT05 в сети B10 после задвижки Э-08 *)
	(* 10	PT06 в сети B10 после задвижки Э-09 *)
	(* 11	PT07 в сети B10 после задвижки Э-10 *)
	(* 12	N11_TT_B1 подшипника №1 Н1.1 *)
	(* 13	N11_TT_B2 подшипника №2 Н1.1 *)
	(* 14	N12_TT_B1 подшипника №1 Н1.2 *)
	(* 15	N12_TT_B2 подшипника №2 Н1.2 *)
	(* 16	N21_TT_B1 подшипника №1 Н2.1 *)
	(* 17	N21_TT_B2 подшипника №2 Н2.1 *)
	(* 18	N22_TT_B1 подшипника №1 Н2.2 *)
	(* 19	N22_TT_B2 подшипника №2 Н2.2 *)
	(* 20	N31_TT_B1 подшипника №1 Н3.1 *)
	(* 21	N31_TT_B2 подшипника №2 Н3.1 *)
	(* 22	N32_TT_B1 подшипника №1 Н3.2 *)
	(* 23	N32_TT_B2 подшипника №2 Н3.2 *)
	(* 24	N11_TE_BM1 подшипника эд №1 Н1.1 *)
	(* 25	N11_TE_BM2 подшипника эд №2 Н1.1 *)
	(* 26	N12_TE_BM1 подшипника эд №1 Н1.2 *)
	(* 27	N12_TE_BM2 подшипника эд №2 Н1.2 *)
	(* 28	N21_TE_BM1 подшипника эд №1 Н2.1 *)
	(* 29	N21_TE_BM2 подшипника эд №2 Н2.1 *)
	(* 30	N22_TE_BM1 подшипника эд №1 Н2.2 *)
	(* 31	N22_TE_BM2 подшипника эд №2 Н2.2 *)
	(* 32	N31_TE_BM1 подшипника эд №1 Н3.1 *)
	(* 33	N31_TE_BM2 подшипника эд №2 Н3.1 *)
	(* 34	N32_TE_BM1 подшипника эд №1 Н3.2 *)
	(* 35	N32_TE_BM2 подшипника эд №2 Н3.2 *)
END_FOR;
(* Discretes *)
FOR i := 1 TO 35 DO
	_gDI(i);
	(* 1	Наличие питания на вводе 1 *)
	(* 2	Наличие питания на вводе 2 *)
	(* 3	Состояние двери АПТ 5 *)
	(* 4	LS01.1 Мин ур в пожарн резервуаре ПР1 *)
	(* 5	LS01.2 Норм ур в пожарн резервуаре ПР1 *)
	(* 6	LS01.3 Макс ур в пожарн резервуаре ПР1 *)
	(* 7	LS02.1 Мин ур в пожарн резервуаре ПР2 *)
	(* 8	LS02.2 Норм ур в пожарн резервуаре ПР2 *)
	(* 9	LS02.3 Макс ур в пожарн резервуаре ПР2 *)
	(* 10	Перегрев обмоток э-д насоса Н1.1 *)
	(* 11	Перегрев обмоток э-д насоса Н1.2 *)
	(* 12	Перегрев обмоток э-д насоса Н2.1 *)
	(* 13	Перегрев обмоток э-д насоса Н2.2 *)
	(* 14	Перегрев обмоток э-д насоса Н3.1 *)
	(* 15	Перегрев обмоток э-д насоса Н3.2 *)
	(* 16	Пожар в резервуарном парке *)
	(* 17	Пожар на эстакаде *)
	(* 18	Пожар в ПНС *)
	(* 19	Пожар общий *)
	(* 20	Неисправность ПС *)
	(* ШУПН 1 *)
	(* 21	Реле защиты от сухого хода *)
	(* 22	Реле давления основное *)
	(* 23	Реле давления резервное *)
	(* 24	Реле перепада давления основное *)
	(* 25	Реле перепада давления резервное *)
	(* ШУПН 2 *)
	(* 26	Реле защиты от сухого хода *)
	(* 27	Реле давления основное *)
	(* 28	Реле давления резервное *)
	(* 29	Реле перепада давления основное *)
	(* 30	Реле перепада давления резервное *)
	(* ШУПН 3 *)
	(* 31	Реле защиты от сухого хода *)
	(* 32	Реле давления основное *)
	(* 33	Реле давления резервное *)
	(* 34	Реле перепада давления основное *)
	(* 35	Реле перепада давления резервное *)
END_FOR;
(* Discrete outputs *)
FOR i := 1 TO 3 DO
	_gDO(i);
	(* 1	HL01 Св-зв сигнализ на ПГСК-02-24 *)
	(* 2	HL02 Св-зв сигнализ на ПГСК-02-24 *)
	(* 3	HL03 Св-зв сигнализ на ПГСК-02-24 *)
END_FOR;
END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)


(* ROUTINE 05: _5_RTU_Master *)

PROGRAM _5_RTU_Master

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First OR sInit THEN
	sInit := FALSE;
	
	(* 4 identical valves on Port 1; 1 6-channel COBA on Port 2 *)
	(* available: 8 request groups on each port, 16 requests in each group *)
	sMaxReqs := 16;
	sMaxCtrls := (4 * 2) / sMaxReqs; (* 24 = Number of Modbus Devices *)
	(* Preparing configs for requests *)
	FOR i := 1 TO 4 DO
		(* reading *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 1] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 1] := 3;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 1] := 4; (* 40004; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 1] := 4;
		(* writing *)
		cfg_SID[i*2 - 0] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg_FUN[i*2 - 0] := 16;
		cfg_ADR[i*2 - 0] := 2; (* 40002; *)
		cfg_LEN[i*2 - 0] := 2;
	END_FOR;
	FOR i := 1 TO 1 DO
		(* reading *)
		cfg2_SID[i*2 - 1] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg2_FUN[i*2 - 1] := 3;
		cfg2_ADR[i*2 - 1] := 3; (* 40003..40014; *)
		cfg2_LEN[i*2 - 1] := 6;
		(* writing *)
		cfg2_SID[i*2 - 0] := ANY_TO_BYTE(i);
		cfg2_FUN[i*2 - 0] := 16;
		cfg2_ADR[i*2 - 0] := 2; (* 40002; *)
		cfg2_LEN[i*2 - 0] := 1;
	END_FOR;
	(* Configuring requests *)
	sOffset := 0;
	sReqNo := 1;
	sCtrlNo := 0;
	FOR i := 1 TO 4 DO
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].single_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].on_modify_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].do_single_req := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.timeout_ms := 1000;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.repeat_over_scan := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_id 			:= cfg_SID[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.func_code 		:= cfg_FUN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_addr	:= cfg_ADR[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_length	:= cfg_LEN[i];
		_Ctrl_1[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.offset := sOffset;
		sOffset := sOffset + cfg_LEN[i];
		sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
		IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
			sReqNo := 1;
			sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
			IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
				sCtrlNo := 0;
			END_IF;
		END_IF;
	END_FOR;
	sOffset := 0;
	sReqNo := 1;
	sCtrlNo := 0;
	FOR i := 1 TO 2 DO
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].enable := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].single_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].on_modify_request := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].do_single_req := FALSE;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.timeout_ms := 1000;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.repeat_over_scan := TRUE;
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_id 			:= cfg_SID[i];
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.func_code 		:= cfg_FUN[i];
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_addr	:= cfg_ADR[i];
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.slave_data_length	:= cfg_LEN[i];
		_Ctrl_2[sCtrlNo].Requests[sReqNo].command.offset := sOffset;
		sOffset := sOffset + cfg_LEN[i];
		sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
		IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
			sReqNo := 1;
			sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
			IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
				sCtrlNo := 0;
			END_IF;
		END_IF;
	END_FOR;
	_Control_1 := 1;
	_Control_2 := 1;
END_IF;

(* Every Cycle *)
sCtrlNo := 0;
sReqNo := 1;
(* 11 valves on Port 1 *)
FOR i := 1 TO 4 DO
	j:=i;
	sCOBA_VLV(
		iReqStatus	:= _Diag_1[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
		i40004 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 1],
		i40005 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 2],
		i40006 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 3],
		i40007 	:= _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 4],
		g		:= j,
		o40002	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 5],
		o40003	=> _HR_1[0].regs[(i-1)*6 + 6]
	);
	sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
	IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
		sReqNo := 1;
		sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
		IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
			sCtrlNo := 0;
		END_IF;
	END_IF;
END_FOR;
(* 1 6-channel COBA on Port 2 *)
FOR i := 1 TO 1 DO
	FOR j := 1 TO 6 DO
		sCOBA_DIS(
			iReqStatus	:= _Diag_2[sCtrlNo].RequestsAndStats[sReqNo].status_request,
			iBit		:= j-1,
			iStatus 	:= _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*13 + j],
			g			:= i*j + 15,
			o40002		=> _HR_2[0].regs[(i-1)*13 + 13]
		);
	END_FOR;
	sReqNo := sReqNo + 1;
	IF sReqNo > sMaxReqs THEN 
		sReqNo := 1;
		sCtrlNo := sCtrlNo + 1;
		IF sCtrlNo > sMaxCtrls THEN 
			sCtrlNo := 0;
		END_IF;
	END_IF;
END_FOR;
END_PROGRAM
(* -------------------- *)


(* ROUTINE 06: _5_TCP_Master *)

PROGRAM _5_TCP_Master

(* First Cycle *)
IF NOT First THEN
	_Control := 1;
	(*	En	IP					Port	ID	Func	RegNo			Len		Offset in _HR	Timeout	*)
	rq01(1,	'192.168.0.1'	,	502	,	1,	3	,	1			,	1	,	2000			,	1000);	_Ctrl[0].Requests[ 3] := rq01.out;	(* АПТ 1.1.1 основной *)

		CON5_111 := 0;

			Timer[sReqNo] := Timer[sReqNo] + CYCLE_TIME;

		END_IF;

	END_IF;
END_IF;
